   King a1 to a2: Rook b2 attacks a2.
   King a1 to b1: Rook b2 attacks b1.
   King a1 to b2: Rook b2 is there.
   YES! It's stalemate.

Of course, Black could just play ...Qa1# or something else to win.
But this gives White a "drawing chance" if Black blunders.
----- END REASONING (Player_White) -----
